using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

[ApiController]
[Route("api/authors")]
public class AuthorsController : ControllerBase
{
    private readonly AppDbContext _db;
    public AuthorsController(AppDbContext db) => _db = db;

    [HttpGet("{id:guid}")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Get(Guid id, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var data = await _db.Authors.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(a => a.Id == id)
            .Select(a => new
            {
                a.Id,
                a.Name,
                LastModified = EF.Property<DateTimeOffset>(a, "LastModified")
            })
            .SingleOrDefaultAsync(ct);

        if (data is null) return NotFound();

        Response.Headers.LastModified = data.LastModified.UtcDateTime.ToString("R");

        // Basic If-Modified-Since check (seconds precision)
        if (Request.Headers.IfModifiedSince.Count > 0 &&
            DateTimeOffset.TryParse(Request.Headers.IfModifiedSince.ToString(), out var ims))
        {
            var lmSeconds = new DateTimeOffset(
                data.LastModified.UtcDateTime.AddTicks(-(data.LastModified.UtcDateTime.Ticks % TimeSpan.TicksPerSecond)),
                TimeSpan.Zero);

            if (lmSeconds <= ims.ToUniversalTime())
                return StatusCode(StatusCodes.Status304NotModified);
        }

        return Ok(new { data.Id, data.Name });
    }
} ^D7657KNv

type Primitive =
  | string
  | number
  | boolean
  | bigint
  | symbol
  | null
  | undefined;

// DeepPartial that:
// - leaves functions as-is
// - handles arrays/tuples
// - handles Map/Set
// - leaves Date as Date
export type DeepPartial<T> =
  T extends Primitive ? T :
  T extends Date ? T :
  T extends (...args: any[]) => any ? T :
  T extends Map<infer K, infer V> ? Map<DeepPartial<K>, DeepPartial<V>> :
  T extends Set<infer U> ? Set<DeepPartial<U>> :
  T extends ReadonlyArray<infer U> ? ReadonlyArray<DeepPartial<U>> :
  T extends Array<infer U> ? Array<DeepPartial<U>> :
  T extends object ? { [K in keyof T]?: DeepPartial<T[K]> } :
  T; ^02ikDKXW

import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useDispatch, useSelector } from "react-redux";

import { pizza_order, pizza_add } from "./features/pizza/pizzaSlice";
import { burger_orderAmount } from "./features/burger/burgerSlice";
import { fetchProducts } from "./features/product/productThunks";

import {
  selectPizzaBase,
  selectBurgerBuns,
  selectProductLoading,
  selectProductError,
  selectProductTitles,
} from "./features/selectors";

export default function App() {
  const dispatch = useDispatch();

  // selectors keep component clean
  const pizzaBase = useSelector(selectPizzaBase);
  const burgerBuns = useSelector(selectBurgerBuns);

  const loading = useSelector(selectProductLoading);
  const error = useSelector(selectProductError);
  const products = useSelector(selectProductTitles);

  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(2);

  useEffect(() => {
    dispatch(fetchProducts()); // triggers pending -> fulfilled/rejected
  }, [dispatch]);

  return (
    <div style={{ padding: 16 }}>
      <h2>Pizza base: {pizzaBase}</h2>
      <button onClick={() => dispatch(pizza_order())}>Order Pizza</button>
      <button onClick={() => dispatch(pizza_add(5))}>Add +5 Pizza Base</button>

      <hr />

      <h2>Burger buns: {burgerBuns}</h2>
      <input
        type="number"
        min={1}
        value={amount}
        onChange={(e) => setAmount(Number(e.target.value))}
      />
      <button onClick={() => dispatch(burger_orderAmount(amount))}>
        Order {amount} Burger(s)
      </button>

      <p style={{ marginTop: 10 }}>
        Ordering a pizza also decreases burger buns (burger listens to pizza
        action via extraReducers).
      </p>

      <hr />

      <h2>Products (Async Thunk)</h2>
      <button onClick={() => dispatch(fetchProducts())}>Refetch</button>

      {loading && <p>Loading...</p>}
      {error && <p>Error: {error}</p>}

      {!loading && !error && (
        <div>
          {products.map((title) => (
            <p key={title}>{title}</p>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
} ^BIwrVTJs
